We introduce SPLIT, a 500-prompt benchmark designed to evaluate LLM consistency in generating emotionally grounded responses across five categories: Stress, Panic, Loneliness, Internal Displacement, and Tension. We evaluate three technically diverse LLMs across three dimensions: Empathetic Accuracy, Linguistic Naturalness, and Contextual & Cultural Grounding. The framework aims to assess and compare the quality of LLM responses in both English and Ukrainian languages, as well as to explore the reliability of the LLM-as-a-jury paradigm. Our findings reveal that Gemini-2.5-Flash and LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct degrade when transitioning to Ukrainian, while DeepSeek-V3 remains comparatively stable within our benchmark. We additionally find that human and AI evaluators agree weakly on empathy and naturalness but diverge on cultural grounding. We further argue that producing Ukrainian text is not equivalent to producing Ukrainian emotional support. Our findings may assist in the future development of more culturally tailored benchmark designs, as well as encourage a stronger emphasis on human-centered evaluation.

</details>


### [40] [HaloGuard 1.0: An Open Weights Constitutional Classifier for Multilingual AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02079)
*Navaneeth Sangameswaran,Preetham S,Ashmiya Lenin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HaloGuard 1.0 是一个基于宪法分类器范式的开源输入安全防护模型，以极小的模型规模（0.8B/4B）在多语言提示安全基准上达到SOTA性能，通过46条政策、2940个子类别的自然语言宪法驱动数据生成，并采用一对一配对反事实、双层无害设计和46语言均衡物化策略。0.8B版本以比竞品小30倍以上的参数量达到90.9 F1（FPR 4.3, FNR 9.5），4B版本达到92.1 F1（FPR 3.5），差错分析表明多数漏报实为基准误标。模型已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源安全防护模型要么体积过大，要么在多语言和误报控制方面表现欠佳，缺乏一个高效、精确、多语言友好的输入安全分类器。

Method: 采用宪法分类器范式，构建包含46条政策和2940个子类别的自然语言宪法；通过一配对反事实（固定主题与词汇、翻转意图）生成训练数据；采用双层无害设计分别处理边界和基线误报；在46种语言中实现均衡物化，将语言视为边界两侧的表面形式而非对抗信号。

Result: HaloGuard 1.0-0.8B在7个提示安全基准上平均F1达90.9，FPR 4.3，FNR 9.5，以比竞品（最高27B）小30倍以上的参数量取得最优平均F1；4B变体平均F1达92.1，FPR降至3.5。差错分析表明多数表观漏报实为基准误标。

Conclusion: HaloGuard证明了宪法分类器范式可以在极小模型规模下实现卓越的输入安全防护性能，为开源安全模型提供了高效、可扩展的新方案，同时持续的红队对抗协议进一步提升了防护鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present HaloGuard 1.0, an open-weights implementation of the constitutional-classifier paradigm for input safety. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on English and multilingual prompt-safety benchmarks at roughly one-tenth the model size of current leading open guard models. The safety constitution is the organising structure of the corpus: a natural-language constitution of 46 policies and 2,940 subcategories drives synthetic data generation, with exhaustive one-to-one paired counterfactuals that hold topic and vocabulary fixed while flipping intent, a two-tier harmless design that separately targets boundary and baseline false positives (FPs), and balanced multilingual materialisation across 46 languages that treats language as a surface form appearing on both sides of the boundary rather than as an adversarial signal. Across seven prompt-safety benchmarks, HaloGuard 1.0-0.8B attains the best average F1 (90.9) of any open guard we evaluate, outperforming baselines up to 27B parameters (over 30 times larger) while holding false-positive rate (FPR) to 4.3 and false-negative rate (FNR) to 9.5. The HaloGuard 1.0-4B variant reaches average F1 of 92.1 and FPR of 3.5, spending its extra capacity on precision rather than recall. A structured adjudication of the remaining failures indicates that most apparent missed-harm cases are benchmark mislabels rather than genuine model misses. An always-on adversarial red-teaming protocol continuously hardens the guard against both content-level and agentic attacks. We release the models as open weights.

</details>


### [41] [Unlocking Speech-Text Compositional Powers: Instruction-Following Speech Language Models without Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02214)
*Congrui Du,Yang Zhang,Kaizhi Qian,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechCombine，仅通过30k小时数据的单轮语音预训练和权重组合策略，无需指令微调即可实现语音语言模型的指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLM训练方法复制文本LLM范式，需要大规模语音预训练和指令微调数据，但由于语音序列远长于文本序列，这种策略难以扩展。

Method: 从文本LLM基座模型出发，先对语音话语进行连续预训练得到语音适配模型，然后将该模型权重与指令微调版本和基础版本文本LLM的权重差直接组合。

Result: 该简单组合策略不仅保留了原始文本LLM的知识和能力，还将其有效迁移到语音领域。

Conclusion: 提出了一种避免依赖大规模语音数据的SLM训练新方向。

Abstract: Instruction tuning for speech language models (SLMs) is substantially more challenging than for text-based large language models (LLMs), as it requires learning a new modality and a wide range of speech-specific instructions in addition to those supported by text LLMs. Existing SLM training approaches largely replicate the text LLM training paradigm by synthesizing large-scale speech pre-training and instruction-tuning datasets. However, this strategy is difficult to scale, since speech sequences are significantly longer than text sequences. In this paper, we propose SpeechCombine, an instruction-following speech language model trained without any instruction tuning, using only a single round of speech pre-training on 30k hours of data. Starting from a text LLM base model, we perform continuous pre-training on speech utterances to obtain a speech-adapted model, and then directly combine its weights with the weight difference between the instruction-tuned and base versions of the text LLM. Our results show that this simple combination strategy not only preserves the knowledge and capabilities of the original text LLM, but also effectively transfers them to the speech domain. These findings suggest a new direction for SLM training that avoids reliance on massive speech data.

</details>


### [42] [Challenges and Recommendations for LLMs-as-a-Judge in Multilingual Settings and Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02235)
*A. Seza Doğruöz,Xixian Liao,Verena Blaschke,Jakob Prange,Senyu Li,David Ifeoluwa Adelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了ACL Anthology中650篇提及LLM-as-a-Judge的论文，发现仅33篇关注多语言或低资源场景。通过对这些论文的深入分析，揭示了评估结果不一致、过度信任LLM判断以及依赖单一评判模型等问题，并提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM-as-a-Judge已成为主流的NLG评估范式，但其研究主要集中在英语场景。随着该范式向多语言和低资源语言扩展，LLM在这些语言中的能力受限且缺乏充分的人类验证，亟需系统审视当前实践中的问题。

Method: 对ACL Anthology中650篇提及LLM-as-a-Judge的论文进行系统性筛选，识别出33篇聚焦多语言或低资源场景的论文，并对其评估方法、评判模型选择、验证流程等进行深入分析。

Result: 仅33/650篇论文关注多语言或低资源场景；这些论文存在评估结果不一致、过度信任LLM在非英语场景中的判断、普遍依赖单一评判模型等问题。

Conclusion: 当前LLM-as-a-Judge在多语言和低资源场景下的应用存在显著问题，建议NLP社区在使用时应更加审慎，采用多模型交叉验证等方式提高评估可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant evaluation paradigm for many natural language generation tasks, due to shortcomings of conventional metrics and high correlations with human judgment, albeit mostly in English. There are now attempts to extend LLM-as-a-Judge to multilingual settings including low-resource languages. However, LLMs have limited proficiency in low-resource languages, and there is often no adequate human validation in these settings. To highlight the scope of the problem and current practices, we explore the use of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators in ACL Anthology papers focusing on multilingual settings and low-resource languages across a diverse set of tasks. Out of 650 papers mentioning LLM-as-a-judge, only 33 of them focus on low-resource or multilingual settings. Our in-depth analysis of these papers indicates inconsistent evaluation outcomes, a tendency to overtrust LLM judgments in multilingual settings, and the widespread reliance on a single judge model per study. To help the NLP community further, we conclude with recommendations about how to use LLM-as-a-Judge in multilingual and low-resource settings.

</details>


### [43] [BamiBERT: A New BERT-based Language Model for Vietnamese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02259)
*Dat Quoc Nguyen,Thinh Pham,Chi Tran,Linh The Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BamiBERT，一种针对越南语的新BERT预训练语言模型，在129GB语料上训练20轮，支持2048 tokens上下文长度，无需外部分词，在15项指标中11项达到最佳，3项第二，创下越南语base编码器的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前越南语事实标准文本编码器PhoBERT的关键局限性。

Method: 从零开始在129GB通用领域越南语文本上训练20轮；支持最长2048 tokens的扩展上下文长度；直接操作原始输入，无需外部词分割。

Result: 在8个越南语基准测试的15项指标中取得11项最佳、3项第二，在"base"规模越南语编码器中达到新SOTA，展现出强大的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: BamiBERT在多个维度（上下文长度、无需分词、性能）上超越了PhoBERT，可作为越南语表示学习的新基础模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce BamiBERT, a new BERT-based pre-trained language model for Vietnamese that addresses key limitations of PhoBERT -- the current de facto Vietnamese text encoder. Trained from scratch on a 129GB corpus of general-domain Vietnamese text for 20 epochs, BamiBERT supports an extended context length of up to 2048 tokens and operates directly on raw input, eliminating the need for external word segmentation. Across 8 Vietnamese benchmarks, it achieves the best score on 11 of 15 metrics and the second-best on 3 others, setting a new state of the art among "base"-sized Vietnamese encoders and demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. We release BamiBERT at: https://huggingface.co/Qualcomm-AI-Research/BamiBERT

</details>


### [44] [CheckRLM: Effective Knowledge-Thought Coherence Checking in Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02262)
*Dingling Xu,Ruobing Wang,Qingfei Zhao,Yukun Yan,Zhichun Wang,Daren Zha,Shi Yu,Zhenghao Liu,Shuo Wang,Xu Han,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CheckRLM是一个通过检索增强生成（RAG）在推理过程中及时检查并纠正事实性错误的框架，显著提升了推理语言模型在长程推理任务中的可靠性，且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型（RLMs）在复杂任务中通过扩展推理链提升了性能，但其推理链容易包含事实性错误，尤其是在知识密集型任务中。现有方法缺乏对推理过程中知识不一致性的有效检测与纠正机制。

Method: CheckRLM框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 从推理链中提取事实性声明，识别并定位推理过程中的细微知识不一致性；(2) 当检测到错误时，通过外部知识进行最小代价但精确的修正，确保推理链与正确知识之间的连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CheckRLM显著优于现有基线方法，在长程推理中有效缓解了错误累积，同时降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: CheckRLM通过RAG驱动的实时检测与精确定位修正机制，为提升推理语言模型在知识密集型任务中的可靠性与效率提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) have significantly improved performance on complex tasks by extending the reasoning chain. However, these chains are prone to containing factual errors, particularly in knowledge-intensive tasks. To address this issue, we propose CheckRLM, a framework that improves the reliability of the reasoning process through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) by timely checking and correcting factual errors. Specifically, CheckRLM extracts factual claims from the reasoning chain to identify and localize subtle knowledge inconsistencies during inference. Upon detection of errors, a refinement mechanism performs minimal-cost yet precise corrections by leveraging external knowledge, ensuring coherence between the reasoning chain and correct knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CheckRLM substantially outperforms existing baselines, exhibiting a strong capability to mitigate error accumulation in long-horizon reasoning with lower costs. The code and data are available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/CheckRLM.

</details>

Conclusion: 方向性表示将瓶颈从符号层转移到神经层，神经层性能可通过编码器升级进一步提升，与方向性增益互补。

Abstract: Several SLOG test categories explicitly involve directional distinctions (modifier position shifts, argument extraction positions), yet AM-Parser, the previous SOTA, uses an AM algebra whose operations do not encode direction. We redesign the symbolic backend around CCG directed types (deterministic CKY + single linear decoder, 30K learnable parameters). Under the same BERT-base encoder, the system achieves 75.9$\pm$6.4% LF exact match, surpassing AM-Parser (70.8$\pm$4.3%). Per SLOG's own category groupings, gains are highly directional: the CCG system outperforms AM-Parser on all 5 position-shift categories (+29.9pp), while AM-Parser outperforms on all 6 recursive-depth categories. Replacing the encoder with DeBERTa-v3-large yields 90.7$\pm$4.9%, with the largest encoder gains in recursive-depth categories, complementary to directionality's gains. Directional representations shift the bottleneck from the symbolic layer (AM-Parser's 0% category ceiling) to the neural layer, which improves with encoder upgrades.

</details>

Abstract: LLMs stage a new form of cultural encounter that is massive, automated, and monolingual. Literary disciplines have always negotiated cultural struggles with comparative reading of literature, narratological and poetic analysis, critical theory, world literature, and translation. These tools have now become indispensable for building culturally literate AI. The essay develops a layered framework toward more nuanced textual models and pluralistic interpretations of AI, emphasizing the natural intersections of literature and AI development, connecting current debates in critical theory with structural monolingualism, and suggesting a new application of world literature approaches to address global AI textuality through macrostructure, circulation, and untranslatability.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HULAT2-UC3M在MER-TRANS 2026西班牙语Easy-to-Read翻译任务中的参与情况，提交了三个全自动西班牙语系统：基于LangGraph多智能体工作流的RUN1（44.05 SARI）和RUN2（43.10 SARI），以及基于RigoChat的线性生成-评估-再生基线的RUN3（38.51 SARI）。结果表明信号引导的多智能体路由优于线性再生基线，但添加词汇支持并未自动提升参考指标分数。

Conclusion: 信号引导的多智能体路由（RUN1/RUN2）优于线性再生基线（RUN3）；添加词汇支持并未自动改善参考指标分数；需要进一步的片段级和文档级分析来评估可读性、事实一致性和面向用户的充分性。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [48] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEDIAREF，一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，用于支持可复现、低成本的媒体背景检查（MBC）生成，以解决现有RAG事实核查中依赖昂贵商业搜索API的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的自动事实核查方法通常假设检索到的证据是可靠的，但现实中的信息可能相互矛盾、过时或来自不可靠来源。虽然近期工作提出了通过媒体背景检查（MBC）进行源批判性推理，但生成MBC依赖于昂贵的商业搜索API，限制了可复现性。

Method: 构建MEDIAREF，一个包含200个媒体来源的网络文档知识库；设计可复现的构建和更新方法；评估多种主流LLM在MBC生成任务上的表现。

Result: MEDIAREF能够支持更高质量的MBC生成，在自动评估和定性评估中均表现良好。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF提供了一种可复现、低成本的替代方案，使得媒体背景检查生成更加便捷和可靠，有助于提升RAG事实核查系统的可信度。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NLP研究正从传统ACL等核心期刊向通用机器学习期刊迁移，LLM时代加速了这一趋势：老牌作者在ACL主会份额下降19.2pp，在ML期刊上升8.6pp；新作者在ML期刊首发比例从5%升至21%，且ML期刊存在显著的引用溢价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM兴起后，NLP研究的学科重心是否正从传统NLP核心会议（如ACL）向通用机器学习期刊迁移。

Method: 分析2010-2026年NLP论文数据，比较LLM前后时期；区分老牌作者和新作者（首次以第一作者发表至少3篇NLP主题论文）；采用因果推断技术评估不同期刊类型的引用溢价。

Result: 老牌作者在ACL主会份额下降19.2pp，在Findings分会上升14.8pp，在ML期刊上升8.6pp；新作者在ACL发表比例从84%降至74%，ML期刊从5%升至21%。ML期刊存在显著的引用溢价。

Conclusion: NLP研究正在经历从传统ACL会议向通用ML期刊的显著迁移，ML期刊的引用溢价是驱动此类选择的重要因素。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [50] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判性地指出，NLP 对文化现象的量化并非中立记录，而是通过模型、数据、标注等“装置”参与构建文化现实。作者借用 Karen Barad 的“能动性切割”概念，论证语言模型因已内化大量文化材料，其设计选择从一开始就与被测量的文化现象纠缠在一起。通过三个影视对话案例和对装置本身的三个检视，文章提出一个理论驱动、实证严谨且文化依赖的研究纲领，将每次“能动性切割”视为兼具方法论与伦理性的自觉承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用于量化文化现象，但学界对“什么使这种测量具有文化独特性”缺乏深入反思。本文旨在揭示 NLP 的文化测量并非中立，而是一种物质-话语实践，装置本身参与了文化现实的构成。

Method: 以 Karen Barad 的“能动性切割”为理论框架，通过三个影视对话案例（测量结构、交互与偏离）以及三个对装置本身的检视（文化标记的擦除、对历史材料的调谐、代理工作流中的能动性），进行概念分析与案例论证。

Result: 揭示了语言模型的设计选择（数据、标注、评估）实质上划定了现象与仪器之间的边界，且由于模型已内化大量文化材料，这种边界从一开始就是纠缠的。案例表明，文化测量中的每一次“切割”都涉及方法论与伦理的双重承诺。

Conclusion: 提出一个理论驱动、实证严谨且文化依赖的研究纲领，主张将每次“能动性切割”视为自觉的、兼具方法论与伦理性的选择，从而推动 NLP 在文化研究中的反思性实践。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [51] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过85个Qwen3架构的Transformer LLM（10^18至10^20 FLOPs预算）和35个开源模型（最高70B参数）的缩放定律实验，研究了LLM计算规模与社会模拟保真度（意见建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）的关系。发现多数任务随规模快速提升，但纵向预测和代表性不足的意见缩放较慢，且行为模拟中模型对风险厌恶等认知偏好的校准无法随规模改善，说明规模可提升大多数社会模拟但存在异常值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前LLM的缩放范式是否能够自然提升社会模拟的保真度，还是模拟保真度与通用能力正交，需要额外的研究关注。

Method: 使用缩放定律方法，在三个子领域（意见建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）中，评估85个Qwen3架构LLM（固定计算预算10^18至10^20 FLOPs）和35个更大更强的开源模型（最高70B参数）的表现，分析损失与下游准确率的关系。

Result: 多数意见和行为模拟任务随规模快速提升，尤其是在英语网络语料中代表性好的群体；纵向预测和代表性不足的意见缩放较慢，与MMLU等通用基准相关性低；行为模拟中模型对风险厌恶等认知偏好的校准无法随规模改善，即使微调也无法从0.5B扩展到8B参数。

Conclusion: 规模提升能在大多数场景下改善社会模拟，但在低资源领域改进不可靠，且存在规模无法解决的异常任务（如认知偏差校准）。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [52] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用预训练音频模型从LibriVox有声书中提取声学特征（如语调、节奏、响度），分析其与消费数据（观看率）的关系，发现声学信息本身与图书吸引力显著相关，且不受标题效应影响，并通过专有参与度指标验证了这一发现。这是首个系统性地将叙述质量、类型、标题与有声书消费联系起来的计算研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙述质量对有声书听众的参与度和理解有重要影响，但其效果因类型、标题和受众而异。目前缺乏系统性的计算研究来量化叙述质量如何影响有声书的消费表现。

Method: 从LibriVox中提取声学特征（语调、节奏、响度等），使用预训练音频模型进行分析，并结合消费数据（观看率）及专有参与度指标，探究声学信息、类型、标题与有声书吸引力之间的关系。

Result: 即使在数据有限的情况下，声学信息本身与有声书吸引力之间存在稳健的关联，且这种关联在控制标题效应后依然显著。该结果通过更细粒度的专有参与度指标得到了进一步验证。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性的计算研究，揭示了叙述质量、类型、标题与有声书消费之间的关联，证明了数据驱动方法在改进有声书个性化和叙述者选角方面的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [53] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大视觉语言模型在思维链推理中视觉自反思能力不足的问题，提出VRRL强化学习框架，通过随机掩码轨迹前缀和体验重放缓存回滚两种机制，显著提升模型在分布外视觉场景下的自反思能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在思维链推理中虽然具备自反思能力，但在反思时往往无法正确关注视觉输入，难以将反馈转化为基于视觉的修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VRRL强化学习框架，包含两个组件：1) 随机掩码轨迹前缀，强制模型从中间错误预测中恢复而非仅避免早期错误；2) 从经验重放缓冲区引入缓冲回滚，使模型暴露于需要学习纠正的多样失败状态。

Result: 在表格、图表视觉定位和空间导航任务上，VRRL相比标准强化学习和面向反思的微调基线，在分布外准确率上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过有效利用自反思机制，VRRL能够使模型在分布偏移下保持稳健性能，弥补了现有模型在视觉接地自反思方面的不足。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [54] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长篇电视剧中的说话人识别任务，提出了包含53.2万条标注对话和900多个角色的基准数据集DramaSR-532K，以及基于大型推理模型的多模态工具使用方法DramaSR-LRM，在短句识别等场景显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇电视剧的视频理解极具挑战性，准确将每句台词归因到对应角色（说话人识别）是理解复杂叙事的关键，但现有方法在整合听觉、语言和视觉多模态线索方面尚不充分。

Method: (1) 构建DramaSR-532K大规模基准数据集，包含53.2万条标注对话和900多个角色，涵盖听觉、语言和视觉多模态信息；(2) 提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大型推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据进行高置信度归因。

Result: DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在声学生物特征不可靠的短句识别场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 多模态工具使用结合大型推理模型能有效提升长篇电视剧中的说话人识别性能，数据和代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [55] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LACUNA测试平台，通过将合成PII注入LLM预定义参数，首次实现了对遗忘方法在参数层面是否真正擦除知识的直接评估。实验表明现有SOTA遗忘方法虽然在输出层面表现良好，但参数层面精度低且易遭受重现攻击；而精确定位后，即使是简单梯度方法也能实现可靠擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法仅从输出层面评估效果，无法确认是否真正从模型参数中擦除了知识还是仅仅掩盖了知识，且重现攻击的成功进一步加剧了这一担忧。因此亟需一种能在参数层面提供真实基准的测试平台。

Method: LACUNA通过掩码持续预训练将合成个体的PII注入到1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，从而获得参数级别的真实标注（ground-truth）。在此基础上评估现有SOTA遗忘方法在参数定位精度和抗重现攻击能力方面的表现。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法在输出层面表现良好，但在参数层面精度极低，且易遭受重现攻击。而精确定位后，即使是简单基于梯度的遗忘方法也能实现强擦除效果和抗重现攻击能力。

Conclusion: 精确的参数定位是实现可靠遗忘的关键，LACUNA为弥补行为评估的不足、推动基于定位的鲁棒遗忘研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [56] [HYPIC: Accelerating Hybrid-Attention LLM Serving with Position-Independent Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01299)
*Yifei Liu,Juntong Wu,Yang Liu,Junhao Hu,Minghao Li,Xiaoxu Chen,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Hypic 是首个支持位置无关缓存（PIC）的混合注意力LLM服务系统。它通过为线性注意力层引入段累积转移算子（SCTO）实现近乎精确的常量时间状态组合，对全注意力层仅需在段边界重计算小窗口来恢复跨段回看，并利用段级自包含特性并行化缓存未命中预填充。在四个混合注意力模型和五个工作负载上，TTFT平均降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐量提升2.0倍，精度损失控制在3.3个点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG和智能体LLM服务中，提示词由独立片段组装成长上下文，导致预填充阶段成为单次请求的计算瓶颈。已有两条并行方向——位置无关缓存（PIC）实现非连续片段间的KV缓存复用，以及混合注意力模型通过线性注意力降低计算复杂度——但二者无法共存：将PIC应用于混合注意力模型会失效，因为每token的KV缓存复用原语无法迁移到每请求的循环状态上。

Method: 1) 在线性注意力层识别段累积转移算子（SCTO）作为缺失的代数原语，并将其与每个片段的零起点终点状态一起缓存，实现独立缓存片段的近乎精确、常数时间状态组合。2) 对全注意力层，发现注意力偏差集中在段边界，仅需在每个边界重计算一个小窗口即可恢复跨段回看。3) 利用段级自包含性，将缓存未命中的预填充跨实例并行化，将长冷请求转化为可加速的工作负载。

Result: 在四个混合注意力模型和五个工作负载上，Hypic将首token延迟（TTFT）平均降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐量提升最多2.0倍，精度与全重计算相比损失在3.3个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Hypic成功弥合了位置无关缓存和混合注意力模型之间的鸿沟，展示了两种技术可以协同工作。其关键创新在于为线性注意力层找到了正确的代数原语（SCTO），并巧妙地利用段边界重计算来处理全注意力层的兼容性问题，同时通过并行化冷请求预填充进一步优化了尾部延迟。这为构建更高效的RAG和智能体LLM服务系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: In retrieval augmented generation (RAG) and agentic LLM serving, prompts are assembled from independent segments into long contexts, making the prefill stage dominate the per-request computation cost. To this cost, two directions have emerged in parallel: position-independent caching (PIC) admits KV reuse for non-contiguous segments shared across different requests, while hybrid-attention models reduce computation complexity by replacing most full-attention layers with linear attention. However, they cannot coexist: applying PIC to hybrid-attention models breaks down because per-token KV-cache reuse primitives do not transfer to the per-request recurrent state.
  In this work, we present Hypic, the first serving system for hybrid-attention LLMs with position-independent caching. For linear-attention layers, we identify the segment-cumulative transition operator as the missing algebraic primitive, and cache it alongside each segment's zero-start end-state, enabling near-exact and constant-time state composition of independently cached segments. For the remaining full-attention layers, existing PIC methods also fail as linear layers do not expose the per-token hidden states for selective recomputation. We show that the most significant attention deviation concentrates at segment boundaries, so recomputing only a small seam window at each boundary suffices to restore cross-segment lookback. Finally, Hypic exploits segment-level self-containment to parallelize cache-miss prefill across instances, turning long cold requests -- a major tail-latency contributor under both prefix caching and prior PIC -- into an accelerable workload. Evaluated across four hybrid-attention models and five workloads, Hypic reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 2.45x on average and improves peak throughput by up to 2.0x over existing systems, while staying within 3.3 points of full-recompute accuracy.

</details>


### [57] [SLFS: a Flexible, Low-Cost Distributed File System Using Serverless Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01486)
*Cheng Hao,Yang,Paola Alsharabaty,Soufiane Jounaid,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Ji-Yong Shin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SLFS是基于无服务器函数构建的分布式文件系统，支持数据和元数据操作，通过多线程短生命周期服务器设计克服冷启动问题，相比λFS、EFS和Ceph分别降低63%、68%和63%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式文件系统按峰值需求配置资源，但文件访问模式波动大，导致非高峰时段大量容量闲置；现有弹性机制以整个服务器为粒度且耗时数分钟，无法跟踪文件系统常见的快速细粒度负载变化。

Method: SLFS在键值存储上实现文件服务，保持函数操作简单且短小；提出多线程短生命周期服务器设计克服冷启动问题；设计策略执行协调器高效映射文件到函数实例、弹性伸缩并控制函数生命周期以平衡性能与成本；支持多种存储后端（如S3、用户管理的键值存储）。

Result: SLFS相比基础无服务器设计将冷启动减少580倍，相比λFS、EFS和Ceph分别以最高63%、68%和63%更低的成本实现更优性能。

Conclusion: 无服务器计算的高弹性（毫秒级）和按需付费模式为分布式文件系统提供了有效替代方案，SLFS证明了此路可行，能在性能与成本间灵活权衡。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed file systems must provision resources for peak demand, yet file access patterns fluctuate significantly, leaving substantial capacity idle during off-peak periods. Existing scaling mechanisms operate at the granularity of entire servers and take minutes to hours, making them unable to track the rapid, fine-grained load variations that file systems commonly experience. Serverless computing, with its millisecond-granularity elasticity and pay-per-use pricing, offers a compelling alternative. We present SLFS, the first distributed file system built with serverless functions for both data and metadata operations. SLFS implements file services on top of key-value stores, keeping function operations simple and short, and introduces a novel multi-threaded, short-lived server design that overcomes the cold-start problem while maintaining low cost. A policy-enforcing coordinator efficiently maps files to function instances, scales the system elastically, and controls function lifetimes to balance performance and cost. SLFS can flexibly run on diverse storage backends -- from cloud-native services like S3 to user-managed key-value stores -- enabling configurable cost-performance trade-offs. Our evaluation shows that SLFS mitigates cold starts by 580$\times$ compared to the base serverless design and outperforms $λ$FS, EFS, and Ceph at up to 63%, 68%, and 63% lower cost, respectively.

</details>


### [58] [OmniPilot: An Uncertainty-Aware LLM Inference Advisor for Heterogeneous GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01579)
*D. Balamurugan,Thomas W. Bush*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OmniPilot是一个面向异构GPU集群的LLM服务启动顾问，通过共形校准的分位数成本模型和OOD弃权层，为配置选择提供预测和推荐，在A100/H100/H200上实现6.2%的吞吐量MAPE和95%的top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享异构GPU集群上部署LLM时，用户和运维人员需要选择GPU类型、张量并行度和精度，但有效吞吐量、启动成功率、集群需求和利用率持续波动，静态配置无法捕捉量化与模型家族的交互、KV缓存压力下的规模-精度权衡以及不同张量并行度下失败率差异超过两倍等关键交互效应，且集群资源常受不可预测硬件故障影响。

Method: OmniPilot采用共形校准的分位数成本模型（覆盖八个服务目标）与基于分布外检测的弃权层相结合，利用经济效用指标对配置进行排序，该指标经过校准以反映运维人员的显示偏好。

Result: 在A100/H100/H200上跨四种精度的460次基准测试中，聚合吞吐量预测MAPE为6.2%，对数空间R²=0.92；top-1准确率95%，平均效用遗憾仅0.003；在OOD保留集上预测误差升至24-46%，但弃权层成功标记所有五个为低置信度。

Conclusion: OmniPilot通过共形校准和OOD弃权机制有效处理异构集群中的动态配置选择问题，并支持通过持续集成OOD场景来扩展支持范围。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) on a shared, heterogeneous GPU cluster requires users and operators to select the GPU type, tensor-parallel degree, and precision before committing valuable node-hours. Making these choices is challenging because effective throughput, launch-success rates, and cluster demand and utilization continuously fluctuate. Furthermore, static configuration recipes miss critical interactions: quantization effects depend heavily on the model family, key-value cache pressure creates size-by-precision trade-offs, and failure rates vary by more than twofold across different tensor-parallel degrees. Additionally, cluster resources are frequently constrained by unpredictable hardware failures. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{OmniPilot}, a launch advisor that predicts serving costs for feasible configurations and abstains when requests fall outside its measured support envelope. OmniPilot pairs a conformally calibrated quantile cost model (spanning eight serving targets) with an out-of-distribution (OOD) abstention layer. It ranks configurations using an economic utility metric calibrated to an operator's revealed preferences. In evaluations across 460 benchmark runs on A100, H100, and H200 hardware across four precisions, OmniPilot predicts aggregate throughput with a 6.2\% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) and a log-space $R^2=0.92$. The advisor achieves 95\% top-1 accuracy with a mean utility regret of just 0.003. When tested on an OOD holdout of unsupported cells, prediction error climbs to 24-46\% and conformal intervals cover 0 of 5 points; however, the abstention layer successfully flags all five as low-confidence. Over time, these OOD scenarios will be integrated into the training dataset to continuously expand the advisor's support envelope.

</details>


### [59] [Arachne: Orchestrating Cascades for Efficient Text-to-Video Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01701)
*Peng Yu,Yuankai Fan,Yang Qiu,Tian Li,Bihuan Chen,Yin Chen,Qizhen Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Arachne是一个新型T2V模型训练框架，通过将训练分解为细粒度计算单元（cascades）并优化分布式执行与同步，相比现有框架将迭代时间降低高达65%，且性能优势随规模增长而放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型训练采用bucketing策略处理视频数据的异构性（不同分辨率、时长），但在静态并行方案（数据并行、序列并行）下难以随计算和数据量扩展，导致严重的工作负载不均衡和硬件利用率低下。

Method: 将训练过程分解为细粒度计算单元（cascades），通过协调的空间和时间优化，在集群中编排这些单元的分布式执行与同步。

Result: 相比领先框架，迭代时间降低高达65%，且性能优势随训练规模扩大呈现正向扩展趋势。

Conclusion: Arachne通过细粒度分解与时空协调优化，有效解决了大规模T2V训练中的负载不均衡问题，实现了高效的规模化训练。

Abstract: The rising demand for AI-generated videos is fueled by advances in large-scale Text-to-Video (T2V) models, trained on extensive datasets of video clips spanning diverse resolutions and durations. To address this data heterogeneity, current training methods often use a bucketing strategy that groups samples into discrete buckets for efficiency. However, this approach struggles to scale with compute and data volumes under static parallelism schemes, such as data and sequence parallelism, leading to significant workload imbalances and hardware under-utilization.
  In this paper, we present Arachne, a novel training framework for efficient T2V model training at scale. Arachne decomposes the training process into fine-grained computational units, called \textit{cascades}, orchestrating their distributed execution and synchronization across the cluster through coordinated spatial and temporal optimization. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Arachne reduces iteration time by up to 65\% over leading frameworks, exhibiting a positive scaling trend where its performance advantages amplify as training scale grows.

</details>


### [60] [Exploiting Task-Based Parallelism for the Red-Black Gauss-Seidel Method on 2D Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01735)
*Shiting Long,Gustavo Ramirez-Hidalgo,Andreas Frommer,Dirk Pleiter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于任务编程模型的红黑高斯-赛德尔迭代法，通过细粒度并行取代传统的分治并行策略，在求解2D泊松方程时实现了与经典方法相当的性能，同时展现出对硬件异步性更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分治并行策略实现多色高斯-赛德尔方法存在全局同步开销大和负载不均衡的问题，需要探索更高效的并行化方案。

Method: 采用两种基于任务的编程模型实现红黑高斯-赛德尔方法，并与经典的分治并行实现进行对比，以2D泊松方程求解为基准测试。

Result: 基于任务的实现能够达到与经典分治并行方法相当的性能，并且在硬件级异步性环境下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于任务的细粒度并行模型可以有效替代传统分治策略，消除全局同步瓶颈，并且该方法可自然推广到非结构化网格和更宽模板的通用多色方案中。

Abstract: Gauss-Seidel is a well-established iterative method for the solution of linear systems, and multicoloring has been widely used to increase parallelism in iterative solution techniques. Implementing multi-color Gauss-Seidel with conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization strategies, however, may be inefficient due to global synchronization requirements and load imbalances. Task-based programming models can mitigate these issues by enabling fine-grained parallelism, removing global barriers and allowing updates of different colors to partially overlap in time. In this work, we implement the red-black Gauss-Seidel method using two task-based programming models and compare them with a classical divide-and-conquer parallel implementation to evaluate the impact of fine-grained parallelism on execution efficiency. The red-black scheme serves as a representative example, as task-based approaches naturally extend to more general multi-color schemes arising from unstructured grids and wider stencils. Using the solve of the 2D Poisson equation as benchmark, our results show that task-based implementations can achieve performance comparable to conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization while providing greater resilience to hardware-level asynchronicity.

</details>


### [61] [HCMS: Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline for Communication-Computation Overlap in Long-Sequence Parallel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01817)
*Chao Yuan,Pan Li,Yingnan Sun,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HCMS通过将多头注意力中的注意力头分块，并利用双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠，从而加速中长序列处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于all-to-all的序列并行方法在处理中长序列时，通信和计算严格串行执行，导致硬件资源利用率不足。

Method: 利用多头注意力的计算独立性，将注意力头划分为多个块，并通过双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠。

Result: 在2-8 GPU规模、31K-56K token的典型视频生成序列长度下，相比Ulysses基线加速10%-17.5%，相比Ring Attention加速5%-14.5%；在Wan2.2模型上实现6.8%的端到端加速。

Conclusion: HCMS的优势与通信比ρ正相关，建议在ρ>20%时使用。

Abstract: All-to-all based sequence parallelism methods execute communication and computation strictly in serial when processing medium-long sequences, resulting in hardware resource underutilization. This paper proposes Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS), which exploits the computational independence of multi-head attention by partitioning attention heads into multiple chunks and achieving fine-grained communication-computation overlap through dual CUDA streams. HCMS is orthogonally compatible with existing optimizations such as FlashAttention and SDPA, requires no modification to underlying kernels, supports uneven partitioning while maintaining numerical equivalence. Experiments validate the effectiveness across four GPU platforms at 2-8 GPU scales: for typical video generation sequence lengths of 31K-56K tokens, HCMS achieves 10\%-17.5\% speedup over the Ulysses baseline and 5\%-14.5\% speedup over Ring Attention; end-to-end acceleration of 6.8\% is achieved on the Wan2.2 model. Theoretical analysis shows that HCMS benefits are positively correlated with communication ratio $ρ$, and its use is recommended when $ρ>20\%$.

</details>


### [62] [Lynx: Progressive Speculative Quantization for accelerating KV Transfer in Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01831)
*Wenchen Han,Gingfung Matthew Yeung,Marco Barletta,William Toner,Amory Hoste,Adam Barker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lynx系统通过将KV缓存划分为高优先级的Anchor流（包含最高有效位）和低优先级的Residual流（剩余精度），实现渐进式、分离传输的KV缓存传输。解码在接收Anchor流后立即开始，并推测性进行，同时并发传输Residual流，随后通过验证确保与高精度解码等价。相比标准8位KV量化，TTFT提升最高1.43倍，精度相比最先进方法提升最高5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文推理中，分解式推理需要跨网络传输大量KV缓存，解码必须等待传输完成才能开始。现有KV量化技术虽减少了数据量，但无法同时实现低网络暴露延迟和高推理精度。

Method: 1. 观察到KV缓存中不同比特对注意力计算和推理精度的贡献不均衡（最高有效位捕捉注意力粗粒度结构，最低有效位细化精度）。2. 提出将KV缓存划分为Anchor流（最高有效位，高优先级）和Residual流（剩余精度，低优先级）的渐进式分离传输方案。3. 解码在Anchor流到达后立即开始推测性执行，同时并发传输Residual流，最后通过验证保证与高精度解码等价。

Result: 在多种模型和负载下，Lynx实现了与激进4位KV量化相当的TTFT，同时匹配高精度BF16推理的精度。相比标准8位KV量化，TTFT提升最高1.43倍；相比最先进方法，精度提升最高5.1%。

Conclusion: Lynx通过挑战KV缓存必须完全接收后才能使用的假设，利用比特级重要性差异实现渐进式传输和推测解码，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了TTFT，为长上下文分解式推理中的KV缓存传输提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly common in large language model (LLM) serving, driven by retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems. In disaggregated inference, these workloads require transferring large Key-Value (KV) caches across the network, where decoding cannot begin until the transfer completes. Recent KV quantization techniques reduce data volume and alleviate this bottleneck, but existing schemes fail to achieve both low network-exposed latency and high inference accuracy.
  We challenge the assumption that the KV cache is an indivisible unit that must be fully received before use. We leverage the observation that different bits in the KV cache contribute unequally to attention computation and inference precision: the most significant bits capture the coarse structure of attention and the least significant bits refine precision. This property enables partial use of the KV cache during decoding. We present Lynx, a system that enables progressive, split-stream KV transfer by partitioning the KV cache into a high-priority Anchor stream carrying the most significant bits and a low-priority Residual stream carrying remaining precision. Decoding begins upon receipt of the Anchor stream and proceeds speculatively while the Residual stream is transferred concurrently, followed by verification that ensures equivalence to higher-precision decoding.
  Across multiple models and serving workloads, Lynx achieves Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) comparable to aggressive 4-bit KV quantization, while matching the accuracy of high-precision (BF16) inference, improving TTFT over standard 8-bit KV quantization by up to $1.43\times$ and improving accuracy over state-of-the-art by up to $5.1\%$.

</details>


### [63] [Mixture-of-Parallelisms: Towards Memory-Efficient Training Stack for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01844)
*Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Shrey Pandit,Yiran Zhao,Semih Yavuz,Silvio Savarese,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MoP，一种用于MoE模型的内存高效训练框架，通过组合多种并行技术和创新的优化器策略，在不到12个8×H200 GPU节点上实现万亿参数规模、百万上下文长度的无损预训练/微调，相比强优化的FSDP2基线提升4.7-8.2倍每GPU吞吐量，并支持1M token上下文长度（基线在64-128K时已内存溢出）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MoE模型规模增至万亿参数和百万token上下文长度，现有训练框架（如FSDP2）在内存和通信效率上存在严重瓶颈，无法在有限GPU资源下高效训练超大规模模型。

Method: 1) 在MoE训练管线的不同层和阶段组合并专门化多种现有及新颖的并行技术；2) 针对CPU、CPU内存、GPU HBM内存以及CPU-GPU、GPU-GPU、节点间通信带宽的物理约束进行优化；3) 提出一种新颖的优化器步骤策略，实现高吞吐量和内存效率。

Result: MoP在每GPU吞吐量上比强优化的FSDP2基线提升4.7-8.2倍（规模越大差距越明显）；支持高达1M token的上下文长度训练，而基线在64-128K时已内存溢出；仅需不到12个8×H200 GPU节点即可训练万亿参数规模模型。

Conclusion: MoP证明了通过精细化的并行技术组合和优化器步骤优化，可以在有限GPU资源下实现超大规模MoE模型的高效无损训练，显著突破现有框架的内存和吞吐量瓶颈。

Abstract: This paper showcases a memory-efficient training stack for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. It is a training paradigm that combines and specializes various existing and novel parallelism techniques at different layers and stages of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model training pipeline. It leverages these techniques to achieve maximal efficiency given the physical constraints of CPU, CPU memory, GPU HBM memory, and the CPU-GPU, GPU-GPU, and node-node communication bandwidth of the GPU cluster. It also contains a novel strategy for the optimizer step to achieve high throughput and memory efficiency, enabling practitioners to conduct lossless pre-training/fine-tuning of trillion-parameter scale models, at a million context length, with just under 12 8x H200 GPU nodes, with state-of-the-art throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, MoP delivers 4.7x--8.2x higher per-GPU throughput than a strongly-tuned FSDP2 baseline (with the gap widening at larger scale) and sustains training at context lengths up to 1M tokens, where the baseline runs out of memory beyond 64--128K.

</details>


### [64] [Towards Load-Aware Prefill Deflection for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02043)
*Shrikara Arun,Anjaly Parayil,Srikant Bharadwaj,Renee St. Amant,Victor Rühle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动式预填充偏转调度器，允许解码节点以分块预填充步骤的方式处理预填充阶段，从而消除排队和跨节点KV缓存传输，在2P2D A100集群上将P95 TTFT降低最多81%，SLO达标率提升最多79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解耦式LLM服务中，预填充节点在突发、重尾负载下易饱和，而解码节点计算资源未充分利用；预填充执行仅占P95 TTFT的2-23%，排队和跨节点GPU-GPU KV缓存传输占用了大部分延迟。

Method: 提出主动式预填充偏转调度器，让解码节点以分块预填充步骤的方式穿插服务预填充阶段；对每个排队请求，估计其在预填充节点上的TTFT，并在每个解码节点上搜索最大块调度方案，使得解码节点的TBT SLO不受影响，在解码路径有助于尾部延迟时进行偏转。

Result: 在vLLM上实现，使用生产级负载和DeepSeek-V2-Lite评估，P95 TTFT降低最高81%，SLO达标率提升最高79%，每请求路由开销低于毫秒级。

Conclusion: 主动将预填充偏转到解码节点可以有效利用解码节点空闲算力，消除跨节点KV传输，显著降低尾部延迟并提升SLO达标率。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM serving runs prefill and decode on separate GPU pools to keep the two phases from interfering. In practice, this creates a new asymmetry: under bursty, heavy-tailed workloads prefill nodes saturate while decode nodes have compute underutilized, and on a production-style A100 cluster with 2 prefill and 2 decode nodes (2P2D), we find that prefill execution accounts for only 2-23% of P95 Time-to-First-Token (TTFT). Queuing and inter-node GPU-GPU KV-cache transfer account for the rest. We present a proactive prefill-deflecting scheduler that lets decode nodes serve prefill phase of requests as chunked-prefill steps interleaved with their in-flight decode batches. For each queued request, we estimate the TTFT it would see on the prefill node, and on every decode node, search for the largest chunk schedule that keeps in-flight decodes within their Time-Between-Tokens (TBT) SLO and deflect when the decode path helps tail latency. Because the prefill phase of deflected requests runs in place on the decode node, the inter-node KV transfer is eliminated. Implemented on vLLM and evaluated on production-style traces with DeepSeek-V2-Lite, our approach reduces P95 TTFT by upto 81% and raises SLO attainment by upto 79% over state-of-the-art disaggregated schedulers, at sub-millisecond per-request routing cost.

</details>


### [65] [Cadence: Extreme Pipelining with Multiple Concurrent Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02275)
*Fatima Elsheimy,Mohammad Mussadiq Jalalzai,Tobias Klenze,Jovan Komatovic,Mike Setrin,Victor Shoup,Kushal Babel,Lioba Heimbach,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cadence 是一种拜占庭容错的多提议者共识协议，支持任意低的区块间隔、最优容错性和最优快速路径延迟。通过极速流水线化将区块间隔与网络延迟解耦，并引入多并发提议者机制实现短期抗审查性和隐藏性。模拟实验显示在200个验证者、每槽5个提议者配置下，最终确认平均219ms，区块间隔可低至100ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFT共识协议中，单领导者垄断交易包含和排序，导致审查风险；区块间隔受限于网络延迟，难以实现高吞吐低延迟。

Method: 提出极端流水线化（extreme pipelining）框架，将单次共识转化为多轮次协议；设计Chorus（槽共识，最优3轮快速路径确认）和Conductor（协调器，异步下放缓开槽速度）；采用多并发提议者机制保证抗审查性和隐藏性。

Result: 在200个验证者、5个提议者/槽的模拟中，最终确认平均219ms，推测性最终确认167ms；100ms区块间隔下，交易平均等待50ms进入提议。

Conclusion: Cadence首次实现多并发提议者协议在单领导者共识的快速路径延迟下，同时提供短期抗审查性和隐藏性，并满足最优容错性（n=3f+1）。

Abstract: We present Cadence, a Byzantine fault-tolerant multi-proposer consensus protocol with arbitrarily low block intervals, optimal resilience, and optimal fast-path latency. Cadence divides time into equally spaced slots, one block per slot, each finalized in its own consensus instance. Blocks do not build directly on their predecessor, so instances run independently and none waits for an earlier block to finish or propagate; we call this extreme pipelining, decoupling the block interval from network latency. Cadence also removes the single-leader monopoly over transaction inclusion and ordering: under multiple concurrent proposers (MCP), several validators propose for each block, and it guarantees that, under synchrony, a transaction a correct proposer includes cannot be censored or deferred (short-term censorship resistance), and that no proposer can craft its proposal in reaction to the others' (hiding). To realize extreme pipelining, we introduce a general framework that turns any one-shot consensus meeting our slot-consensus specification into a multi-shot protocol. We instantiate it for MCP with two protocols of our own: Chorus, a slot consensus whose fast path finalizes a block in an optimal three rounds, with speculative finality one round earlier, and Conductor, an orchestrator that opens slots at an even cadence, more slowly under asynchrony to keep open slots bounded. To our knowledge, Cadence is the first MCP protocol to provide short-term censorship resistance and hiding at the fast-path latency of single-leader consensus. We prove safety, liveness, censorship resistance, and hiding under partial synchrony with optimal resilience (n = 3f+1). In simulation over Monad's 200 validators with five proposers per slot, finalization averages 219 ms (167 ms to speculative finality); at a 100 ms block interval a transaction waits on average 50 ms to enter a proposal.

</details>


### [66] [Securing People and their Machines Against Major Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02304)
*Ohad Eitan,Idit Keidar,Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对草根平台（由自选公钥标识的人和设备组成的分布式系统）的安全恢复机制，通过社交图谱、身份托管人和状态托管人协同工作，在用户丢失私钥或设备时实现身份和状态恢复，并证明了该机制在可恢复故障下的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草根平台没有全局资源可用于恢复，当用户丢失私钥或手机（即重大故障）时，需要一种去中心化的、基于对等节点的恢复方案来保证系统的安全性和可用性。

Method: 基于三个核心组件：（1）草根社交图谱，用户建立和维护好友关系；（2）身份托管人，由用户指定；（3）状态托管人，平台特定。当身份丢失时，在身份托管人的超多数同意下，好友用新公钥替换旧公钥并恢复友谊关系。丢失设备但未丢失密钥的情况则只需状态托管人协助。论文用守卫多智能体原子事务对社交图谱及其安全版本进行规约，并通过通信意愿智能体实现安全社交图谱，最终证明实现映射了可恢复故障下的正确运行。

Result: 定义了社交图谱的安全版本作为守卫多智能体原子事务，并通过通信意愿智能体在最终同步消息传递模型中实现了该安全社交图谱，证明了该实现能够将可恢复故障的运行映射到规约的正确运行。类似的方法也应用于草根货币和债券的状态恢复，确保单一写入者日志被准确恢复，主权恢复后不会发生双花。

Conclusion: 提出了一种不依赖全局资源的去中心化恢复方案，能够应对私钥丢失和设备丢失两类重大故障，并通过形式化规约和实现证明保证了恢复的正确性，同时可推广到草根货币和债券等应用场景。

Abstract: We consider grassroots platforms -- distributed systems of agents consisting of people identified by self-chosen public keys and their machines (smartphones) -- and wish to make them secure against \emph{major faults}: the loss of their private keys and/or their smartphones. As grassroots platforms have no global resource to rely on for recovery, our peer-based solution is based on: (\ia) \emph{a grassroots social graph} in which agents establish and maintain friendships; (\ib) \emph{identity custodians}, designated by each person, and (\ic) \emph{state custodians}, which are grassroots platform-specific. Upon a person experiencing identity loss, and given a willing supermajority of the identity custodians of the person, the friends of the person replace the old public key with the new one across the graph and restore friendships, where all friends serve as state custodians for the social graph. Choosing a new keypair, obtaining a new smartphone, and convincing identity custodians to will a change of key all happen ``off-chain''. Recovery from machine loss without loss of key (e.g. smartphone run over by truck, or its memory wiped) is simpler, requiring only the help of state custodians.
  We specify the social graph and its secure version as guarded multiagent atomic transactions, and implement the secure social graph via communicating volitional agents, an eventually synchronous message-passing model one step closer to implementation. We prove the implementation maps runs with recoverable faults to correct runs of the specification.
  We follow a similar path for grassroots coins and bonds, showing a common core as well as the platform-specific aspects of state recovery: a currency's single-writer log is recovered exactly, the recovered sovereign resuming without double-spending.

</details>


### [67] [Elasticity in Parallel Sparse Triangular Solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02324)
*Raphael S. Steiner,Christos K. Matzoros,Pál András Papp,Toni Böhnlein,A. N. Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于陈旧同步并行（SSP）模式的稀疏三角线性系统求解并行调度器ElasticDivide，通过重叠同步与计算操作，在ARM和x86多核机器上分别实现了7-30%和19-60%的几何平均加速比，超越了现有最先进的同步调度器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏三角线性系统求解（SpTRSV）中，同步操作限制了并行效率，导致计算资源浪费。作者希望引入陈旧同步并行模式，通过允许一定程度的数据滞后（stale data）来减少同步开销，从而提升并行求解性能。

Method: 提出一种通用的有向无环图（DAG）调度器ElasticDivide，支持陈旧同步并行（SSP）调度策略。该调度器允许计算节点在无需严格全局同步的情况下执行，允许部分线程使用稍旧的数据继续计算，从而让同步与计算操作重叠执行。

Result: 在48核ARM机器上，相比最先进的同步调度器GrowLocal，ElasticDivide实现了7-30%的几何平均加速；在48核x86机器上，相比SpMP实现了19-60%的几何平均加速。

Conclusion: 陈旧同步并行模式在稀疏三角线性系统求解中有效，通过弹性调度策略可以显著提升多核并行效率，支持跨不同硬件架构的性能优势。

Abstract: We introduce stale synchronous parallel as a mode of execution in parallel sparse triangular linear system solve and present a general directed-acyclic-graph scheduler capable of producing such schedules. Stale-synchronous-parallel schedules allow the overlap of synchronisation and compute which results in a geometric-mean speed-up of $7$-$30\%$ of our scheduler, ElasticDivide, over state-of-the-art synchronous scheduler GrowLocal on an ARM machine using 48 cores. On an x86 machine using 48 cores, we report geometric-mean speed-ups of $19$-$60\%$ over SpMP.

</details>


### [68] [WattGPU: Predicting Inference Power and Latency on Unseen GPUs and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02391)
*Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst,Marta Patiño-Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出WattGPU，利用公开的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问即可预测GPU功耗和令牌间延迟，在42个LLM和8个GPU上的评估显示，功耗预测中位绝对百分比误差≤3.4%（离线）和≤13.5%（服务器），延迟预测≤8.5%，相比物理基线方法误差降低约2-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理工作负载在数据中心能耗中占比快速增长，但运营者缺乏有效的工具在无需详尽性能剖析的情况下，将特定LLM与最高效的GPU进行匹配。现有预测模型仍需剖析数据，且难以泛化到训练时未见的硬件。

Method: 提出WattGPU框架，包含两个预测模型分别用于平均GPU功耗和令牌间延迟(ITL)。方法仅依赖公开可用的LLM元数据和GPU规格参数，无需硬件访问或剖析。采用留一GPU法和留一LLM法进行交叉验证，数据集涵盖42个开源LLM（0.1B-27B参数）和8种GPU，覆盖离线和服务器两种场景。

Result: 功耗模型在未见GPU上中位绝对百分比误差离线≤3.4%、服务器≤13.5%；延迟模型在服务器模式下≤8.5%。服务器场景下GPU排名相关性Kendall τ≥0.76。相比基于热设计功耗(TDP)的负载缩放基线和基于屋顶线(roofline)的延迟基线，在未见LLM-GPU组合上误差降低约4倍，在完全未见GPU上降低约2倍。

Conclusion: WattGPU实现了无需硬件访问即可准确预测LLM推理功耗和延迟的目标，具有良好的泛化能力，为数据中心运营者提供了有效的GPU-LLM匹配工具。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference workloads are a rapidly growing contributor to data center energy consumption. Optimizing these deployments requires matching specific LLMs to the most efficient GPUs, but operators currently lack the tools to do so without exhaustively profiling each combination. While some predictive models exist, they still require profiling data and struggle to generalize to hardware unseen during training. To address this, we introduce \textit{WattGPU}, featuring two predictive models for mean GPU power draw and Inter-Token Latency (ITL). Our approach leverages only publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, eliminating the need for hardware access or profiling while enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA server-grade GPUs and LLMs. We evaluate our models using rigorous leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation on a dataset of 42 open-source LLMs (0.1B--27B parameters) and 8 GPUs under both offline and server scenarios. The mean power draw model achieves a median absolute percentage error of $\leq3.4\%$ for offline and $\leq13.5\%$ for server scenarios on unseen GPUs, while the latency model achieves $\leq8.5\%$ in server mode, both maintaining strong GPU ranking correlations for server scenarios (Kendall $τ\geq0.76$). Compared to standard physically grounded baselines -- Load-Scaled Thermal Design Power (TDP) for power draw and roofline for latency -- our models reduce median absolute percentage error by approximately 4$\times$ on unseen LLM-GPU combinations for server scenarios or approximately 2$\times$ for completely unseen GPUs. WattGPU's data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu.

</details>


### [69] [FlintKV: A Fast Durable Storage Engine for Modern Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02401)
*Sergey Egorov,Gregory Chockler,Brijesh Dongol,Dan O'Keeffe,Sadegh Keshavarzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlintKV 是一种基于 NVM 优化的跳表存储引擎，支持生产级键值存储的完整 API（原子批量写入、快照一致性迭代、持久化线性一致性），通过新颖的 flat-combining 并发控制算法和多版本设计，在端到端吞吐量上比现有方案提升高达 75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NVM 键值存储引擎虽然实现了高吞吐的写入和点查，但缺乏生产级数据库所需的关键接口保障（如快照、一致性迭代器、原子批处理），而这些是实现事务和并发控制的必要基础。

Method: 提出基于 flat-combining 的新型并发控制算法，结合多版本机制与精心协同设计的持久化策略，构建 NVM 优化的跳表存储引擎，支持完整的生产级 API。

Result: FlintKV 在端到端吞吐量上相比现有工作实现高达 75% 的提升，同时支持原子批量写入、快照一致性迭代和持久化线性一致性。

Conclusion: FlintKV 证明了 NVM 跳表存储引擎可以在不牺牲生产级接口保障的前提下，实现显著的性能提升，具备独立部署或集成到现有 NVM 存储系统的能力。

Abstract: Byte-addressable non-volatile memory (NVM) offers an opportunity to rethink storage engine architectures. While recent NVM key-value stores achieve high throughput for ingestion and point lookups, they omit or under-specify the support for the richer interface guarantees required by modern databases. Production key-value engines (e.g., RocksDB) provide point-in-time snapshots, consistent iterators, and atomic batches-features essential for implementing transactions and concurrency control. We present FlintKV, an NVM-optimized skiplist-based storage engine that natively supports the full API of production key-value stores. FlintKV supports both atomic batch writes and snapshot-consistent iteration efficiently while guaranteeing durable linearizability. FlintKV can be deployed standalone or its durable skiplist can be integrated into existing NVM stores to enhance their capabilities. Central to FlintKV is a novel flat-combining based concurrency control algorithm that leverages multi-versioning and carefully co-designed persistence mechanisms to ensure high performance and scalability. Our empirical evaluation shows that FlintKV can achieve up to a 75% improvement in end-to-end throughput over prior work.

</details>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Field-Deployable RF Capture System for Indoor, Outdoor, and Foliage Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01368)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Peter B. Foytik,Jared Cochran,Jeff Bobrow,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HackRF One、Raspberry Pi 5和GNSS的便携式电池供电RF捕获系统，可在20 Msps速率下连续记录带地理位置标记的IQ数据。在2.45 GHz的密集植被、城市室外和室内办公室三个场景的现场实验验证了系统性能，展示了不同传播特征，且系统实现了75-85 MB/s无丢样写入和亚秒级GNSS同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF测量平台多采用实验室级设备，成本高昂或依赖固定基础设施，难以在真实环境中进行快速、分布式或长期现场测量，缺乏实用且经济的便携式方案。

Method: 集成HackRF One SDR、Raspberry Pi 5、GNSS接收器、稳压电池供电和高速固态存储，以SigMF格式记录连续IQ数据（最高20 Msps），并在2.45 GHz频段于三种典型环境（密集植被、城市室外、室内办公室）进行现场实验。

Result: 植被环境信号接近噪声基底（-76~-82 dBFS），无明显频谱结构；城市环境呈现30 dB动态范围的多径活动、重叠WiFi信道及ISM频段干扰；室内环境主导WiFi信道明显，建筑穿透损耗约20-25 dB，干扰基底比室外高8-10 dB。系统写入吞吐量75-85 MB/s，无丢样，GNSS同步时间<1秒，定位精度米级。

Conclusion: 便携式低成本SDR平台能够产生高保真、带地理标记的IQ数据集，可用于频谱表征、干扰分析、无线电环境映射及环境感知无线研究。

Abstract: Reliable and reproducible radio-frequency (RF) measurements in real-world environments are essential for characterizing spectrum behavior across unlicensed ISM and WiFi bands, licensed mid-band allocations, and emerging next-generation wireless deployments. Existing measurement platforms are often laboratory-grade, cost-prohibitive, or dependent on fixed infrastructure, limiting their practicality for rapid, distributed, or long-duration field campaigns. This paper presents a compact, battery-powered RF capture system integrating a HackRF One software-defined radio, Raspberry Pi 5, GNSS receiver, regulated battery supply, and high-speed solid-state storage. The platform records continuous IQ data at up to 20 Msps in SigMF format with per-segment location and timing metadata for reproducible spectrum analysis. Field experiments at 2.45 GHz in dense foliage, urban outdoor, and indoor office environments reveal distinct propagation signatures. Foliage measurements remain near the noise floor at -76 to -82 dBFS with limited spectral structure, consistent with strong canopy attenuation. Urban measurements show multipath activity across a 30 dB dynamic range, overlapping WiFi channels, and frequent ISM-band interference. Indoor measurements show dominant WiFi channels, an estimated 20 to 25 dB building entry loss relative to outdoor conditions, and an 8 to 10 dB higher interference floor caused by structural reflections. The system sustained 75 to 85 MB/s write throughput with no dropped samples or buffer underruns, while GNSS synchronization remained below one second with meter-level positioning. These results show that a portable, cost-effective SDR platform can produce high-fidelity, geotagged IQ datasets for spectrum characterization, interference analysis, radio environment mapping, and environment-aware wireless research.

</details>


### [71] [Physically-Aware Preemptive Virtual Channels for Deadlock-Free AXI Networks-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01430)
*Lorenzo Leone,Luca Colagrande,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对多核系统芯片（SoC）中网络片上天线（NoC）在AXI4协议下读写流量依赖导致的死锁问题，评估了四种死锁避免方案，并提出了一种名为Preemptive VCs的轻量级虚拟通道架构，相比多平面方案可节省高达76%的链路资源，仅增加3%的路由器面积开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核SoC规模持续扩大，NoC需要维持高内存带宽并避免死锁。AXI4协议中读写流量间的协议级依赖会形成循环等待，即使路由算法本身无死锁，也需要有效分离流量类别以解除依赖。

Method: 评估了四种死锁避免方案：多平面基线方案与三种轻量级基于虚拟通道（VC）的设计。其中提出的Preemptive VCs是一种物理感知架构，通过硬件优化实现读写流量分离。

Result: Preemptive VCs相比多平面设计可节省76%的链路资源，频率相当，且路由器面积开销仅增加3%。

Conclusion: 针对现代宽链路NoC，重新审视了多平面与VC方案的设计权衡，表明轻量级VC设计（特别是Preemptive VCs）在资源效率和性能之间取得了更优平衡，是实现AXI4死锁避免的有效方案。

Abstract: As many-core Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) continue to scale, Networks-on-Chip (NoCs) must sustain increasingly high memory bandwidth while preserving deadlock freedom. In AXI4 systems, protocol-level dependencies between read and write traffic can create circular waits at the network endpoints, even when the routing algorithm itself is deadlock-free. Decoupling these traffic classes avoids such dependencies, but exposes a key implementation trade-off: multiplane NoCs duplicate wide physical links and increase routing pressure, whereas conventional Virtual Channel (VC) routers add substantial control complexity, area, and timing overhead. This work revisits this trade-off for modern wide-link NoCs. We evaluate four deadlock-free AXI4 traffic-class separation schemes: a multiplane baseline and three lightweight VC-based designs. Among these designs, we propose Preemptive VCs, a physically-aware architecture that can save up to 76% of link resources with comparable frequency and only 3% router area overhead relative to the multiplane design.

</details>


### [72] [MxGLUT: A Reconfigurable LUT-Centric Broadcast Dataflow Accelerator for Mixed-Precision GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01607)
*Weiyu Zhou,Chen Ding,Mingyuan Liu,Liangyu Gan,Yukun Feng,Hao Jia,Haoming Chu,Lirong Zheng,Ning Ma,Yuxiang Huan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MxGLUT，一种基于可重构LUT广播数据流和混合精度LUT处理单元的加速器，统一支持FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8的GEMM计算，无需专用FP乘法器或额外FP数据路径，在28nm工艺下相比FIGLUT基线实现最高56.92%乘法器面积缩减和77.07%功耗降低，在Llama系列模型上取得最高2.16倍预填充和1.49倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LUT加速器主要针对FP8-INT4计算，仍依赖独立FP数据路径处理注意力GEMM，导致硬件冗余和非统一的混合精度执行；此外其静态数据流无法适配LLM推理中预填充和解码两阶段的不同计算特征。

Method: 提出统一的LUT执行框架，通过混合精度LUT处理单元(MxLPE)统一FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8 GEMM；设计可重构LUT广播(RLB)数据流，预填充阶段本地化累加部分和，解码阶段重用权重。

Result: 28nm CMOS 200MHz下，乘法器面积减少56.92%，功耗降低77.07%(FP8-INT4)/78.35%(FP8-FP8)；面积效率0.492 TFLOPS/mm²，能效11.58 TFLOPS/W；Llama系列上预填充和解码延迟加速最高2.16×和1.49×，归一化能耗降至0.44×和0.71×，困惑度增加不超过1.70%。

Conclusion: MxGLUT通过统一LUT计算机制和可重构数据流，有效解决了LLM推理中混合精度加速的硬件冗余和数据流失配问题，在面积、功耗和性能上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference suffers from growing inefficiency across the prefill and decode phases, especially under weight-only quantization, where activations remain in FP8 while weights are compressed to low-bit integers. Existing LUT-based accelerators mainly target FP8-INT4 computation and still rely on separate floating-point (FP) datapaths for attention GEMM operations, leading to redundant hardware and non-unified mixed-precision execution. Moreover, their static dataflows are poorly matched to the distinct prefill and decode phases. To address these challenges, we propose MxGLUT, a reconfigurable LUT-centric broadcast (RLB) dataflow accelerator built on mixed-precision LUT-based processing elements (MxLPEs). Guided by a unified LUT-based execution framework, MxGLUT organizes both FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 GEMMs under a single LUT-based compute mechanism without dedicated FP multipliers or additional FP datapaths, and further adopts the RLB dataflow that localizes heavy partial-sum accumulation during the prefill phase and exploits weight reuse in the decode phase. Synthesized in UMC $28\,\mathrm{nm}$ CMOS at $200~\mathrm{MHz}$, MxGLUT reduces multiplier area by up to $56.92\%$ and power by up to $77.07\%$ and $78.35\%$ in FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 modes, respectively. At the accelerator level, MxGLUT achieves an area efficiency of $0.492~\mathrm{TFLOPS/mm^2}$ and an energy efficiency of $11.58~\mathrm{TFLOPS/W}$, while adding native FP8-FP8 support incurs only $2.57\%$ and $3.34\%$ reductions in area and energy efficiency, respectively, relative to the FP8-INT4-only FIGLUT baseline. Across the Llama family, MxGLUT achieves up to $2.16\times$ and $1.49\times$ latency speedup, and reduces normalized energy to $0.44\times$ and $0.71\times$ in prefill and decode, respectively, with at most $1.70\%$ perplexity increase.

</details>


### [73] [3DLS: A 3D Logic-Stacked Architecture for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01617)
*Jaehun Lee,In-Jun Jung,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出3DLS，一种逻辑-on-逻辑3D堆叠芯粒架构，通过垂直互连分离KV缓存传输与解码侧TP集合通信，解决PD分离LLM服务中混合流量争用问题，实现最高1.49倍吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务中，PD分离与张量并行（TP）结合时，传统2D/2.5D芯粒架构中KV缓存传输和解码侧TP集合通信共享同一横向片间互连，导致混合流量争用，增加通信延迟，延长令牌生成间隔，降低端到端服务性能。

Method: 提出3DLS逻辑-on-逻辑3D堆叠芯粒架构，通过垂直互连路由KV缓存传输，同时保留解码侧TP集合通信在横向D2D互连上，实现流量类别的物理隔离。

Result: 相比共享互连平面基线，3DLS实现最高1.49倍吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低；相比工作负载感知优先级管理的平面基线，仍实现最高1.17倍吞吐量提升和31.4%的端到端延迟降低。

Conclusion: 物理隔离是未来基于芯粒的PD分离LLM服务系统的重要设计原则。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving increasingly combines prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation with tensor parallelism (TP) to support large models and long contexts. In conventional 2D/2.5D chiplet architectures, layer-wise prefill-to-decode KV-cache transfer decode-side TP collectives share the same lateral die-to-die (D2D) interconnect, creating mixed-traffic contention on the decode critical path. This contention increases communication latency, prolongs token generation intervals, and degrades end-to-end serving performance. We propose 3DLS, a logic-on-logic 3D-stacked chiplet architecture that separates traffic classes by routing KV-cache transfers through vertical interconnects while preserving decode-side TP collectives on the lateral D2D fabric. 3DLS achieves up to 1.49$\times$ throughput and 60.2\% lower end-to-end (E2E) latency over the shared-fabric planar baseline, and still achieves up to 1.17$\times$ throughput and 31.4\% lower E2E latency over a workload-aware priority-managed planar baseline. These results highlight that physical isolation is an important design principle for future chiplet-based PD-disaggregated LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [74] [Approximate Attention Weighting for Sustainable FPGA-Based Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01798)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无BRAM的近似注意力加权单元，用于FPGA上的ViT推理。该设计采用16段分段线性函数在分布式LUT上近似自然指数，替代了软max中昂贵的指数计算。在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现，仅使用1444个LUT、77个DSP，无BRAM，硬件仿真显示与精确软max参考相比，top-1准确率差异在0.20%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在小FPGA上部署面临挑战，因为自注意力中的软max阶段需要指数运算和归一化，在硬件中实现成本高昂。现有方案依赖CORDIC流水线或BRAM查找表，增加了面积和功耗。

Method: 提出16段分段线性函数在分布式LUT上近似自然指数，替代软max中的指数运算；保留自然指数形式，避免基2近似所需的模型特定重新校准；整个注意力行核心完全由LUT和DSP构成，不使用BRAM。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现，硬件资源消耗为1444个LUT、77个DSP、0个BRAM；硬件仿真显示与精确软max参考相比，ViT系列模型上top-1准确率绝对差异在0.20%以内。

Conclusion: 证明了所提出的核心在资源受限的边缘AI平台上实现节能ViT推理的潜力。

Abstract: Vision Transformers have reshaped computer vision by using self-attention to capture global context across image regions. This makes them attractive for edge visual inspection and monitoring in applications such as renewable-energy infrastructure, industrial quality control, medical imaging, and autonomous-system sensing. However, deploying ViTs on small FPGAs remains challenging because the softmax stage in self-attention requires exponential evaluation and normalization, which are costly in hardware. Existing implementations often rely on CORDIC pipelines or BRAM-based look-up tables, increasing area and power consumption. This paper presents a BRAM-free approximate attention-weighting unit for FPGA-based ViT inference. The proposed design approximates the natural exponential in softmax using a 16-segment piecewise-linear function implemented entirely with distributed LUT fabric. Unlike base-2 approximations, the natural-exponential formulation preserves the pre-trained attention temperature and avoids model-specific recalibration. Implemented on a Xilinx Zynq-7020, the complete attention-row core uses 1444 LUTs, 77 DSPs, and no BRAM, while hardware-accurate emulation shows accuracy within a \(0.20\%\) absolute top-1 difference from the exact-softmax reference on ViT-family models. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed core for energy-efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge-AI platforms.

</details>


### [75] [Probabilistic Memory for Trustworthy Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02465)
*Likai Pei,Jiahao Zheng,Xueji Zhao,Emilie Ye,Jianbo Liu,Hanqing Tao,Ming-Yen Lee,Ruiyang Qin,Yiyu Shi,Shimeng Yu,X. Sharon Hu,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出概率存储器(p-MEM)，一种统一存储原语，可直接在原生存储带宽下存储分布参数并采样，实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的高斯随机数生成吞吐量，在贝叶斯神经网络任务中降低指令数、延迟和能耗分别达2.19x/4.37x、562x/3.45x和295.5x/3.53x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率计算在可信边缘智能中至关重要，但受限于高斯随机数生成与计算间的数量级数据吞吐量差距以及指令开销，现有方案难以高效支持。

Method: 提出p-MEM统一存储原语，将分布参数（均值、标准差）直接存储在存储器中，并在原生存储带宽下直接采样，确定性数据为零方差的特殊情况。通过布局验证的模拟器探索器件选择、存储规格和技术节点。

Result: p-MEM可实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的GRNG吞吐量（含存储阵列访问）。在CPU/GPU系统中，贝叶斯神经网络任务指令数减少2.19x/4.37x，采样延迟降低562x/3.45x，能耗降低295.5x/3.53x。

Conclusion: p-MEM为可信概率AI提供了可扩展的硬件基板，弥合了随机数生成与计算之间的吞吐量差距。

Abstract: Probabilistic computation plays an important role in trustworthy edge intelligence to quantify uncertainty, enhance robustness, reconstruct data, and protect privacy, but its adoption is limited by the orders-of-magnitude data throughput gap between Gaussian random number generation (GRNG) and computation, as well as instruction overhead. This paper introduces probabilistic memory (p-MEM), a unified memory primitive that stores distribution parameters, such as mean and standard deviation, and samples directly at the native memory bandwidth, where deterministic data becomes the zero-variance special case. Using a layout-validated p-MEM simulator, we comprehensively explore device choices, memory specifications, and technology nodes, showing that p-MEM can achieve more than 1000 GSa/s/mm^2 GRNG throughput, including memory-array access. Integrated into CPU/GPU systems, p-MEM reduces instruction count by up to 2.19x/4.37x, sampling latency by 562x/3.45x, and energy by 295.5x/3.53x for Bayesian neural network workloads, providing a scalable hardware substrate for trustworthy probabilistic AI.

</details>

### [76] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个模块化神经符号框架，通过将预测与符号推理分离，在反事实解释生成中融入领域知识和干预约束，产生可行且可解释的解释。在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能够生成更符合领域可行性要求的解释，但需要在有效性和合理性之间进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但因缺乏显式机制来融入领域知识和干预约束，常产生不现实或不可行的建议。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用模块化神经符号架构，分离为两个组件：神经网络预测模型用于分类，符号推理层（使用回答集编程ASP）在反事实生成过程中强制执行领域特定约束，显式建模可行的干预操作。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的实验表明，符号约束能生成更满足领域可行性要求的解释，揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在实现透明、可行性感知的反事实解释方面具有潜力，所提框架具有模型无关性，可适用于需要现实决策支持的领域。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [77] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个用于联邦学习算法配方搜索的约束编码智能体工作流。智能体可提出并实现包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标和模型变体在内的训练算法，而任务配置文件固定变异表面、计算预算、通信契约和最终模型评估。在五个医疗跨孤岛FLamby任务和五个固定LEAF数据集及LEAF合成任务上进行评估。五轮重复评估支持在四个FLamby任务和五个LEAF配置上获得收益，但也暴露了种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。相同预算的对照实验表明，部分收益对应FL配方变化，而其他改进可通过固定表面标量控制复现或在重复/保留评估中失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中的算法选择（优化器、聚合规则、训练调度等）繁多且相互影响，人工探索成本高昂且难以公平比较不同候选算法的效果。

Method: 提出AFR框架，采用约束编码智能体工作流，智能体可自主提出和实现FL训练算法候选（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标函数、注册模型变体），通过任务配置文件固定变异表面、计算预算、通信契约和模型评估路径，自动搜索FL算法配方。

Result: 在五个FLamby医疗任务中四个取得收益，在六个LEAF配置中五个取得收益；同时发现部分改进源于真正的FL配方变化，部分可通过固定表面标量控制复现，还有部分在重复或保留评估中失效，揭示了搜索中的种子敏感性和失败案例。

Conclusion: AFR能够自动发现有效的FL算法配方，但也暴露出智能体生成候选结果的可重复性问题，需要将真正的FL机制改进与固定表面调优和单次运行伪影区分开来。
